# audio_dsp.py
import numpy as np

class AudioDSP:
    """Zero-dependency, vectorized real-time audio enhancement DSP pipeline."""
    
    FIR_KERNELS = {
        # Light: Cutoff ~12 kHz
        "light": np.array([0.04, 0.11, 0.22, 0.26, 0.22, 0.11, 0.04], dtype=np.float32),
        
        # Medium: Cutoff ~8 kHz (Recommended authentic GBA warm sound)
        "medium": np.array([0.015, 0.045, 0.095, 0.160, 0.210, 0.160, 0.095, 0.045, 0.015], dtype=np.float32),
        
        # Heavy: Cutoff ~5 kHz (Maximum smoothness)
        "heavy": np.array([0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.09, 0.14, 0.19, 0.14, 0.09, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01], dtype=np.float32),
        
        # Bass extraction kernel (~300 Hz low-pass)
        "bass": np.ones(31, dtype=np.float32) / 31.0
    }

    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        self.update_settings()

    def update_settings(self):
        """Refreshes DSP settings directly from the active configuration."""
        self.lpf_mode = str(self.config.get("audio", "low_pass_filter") or "off").lower()
        self.stereo_widener = bool(self.config.get("audio", "stereo_widener"))
        self.bass_boost = bool(self.config.get("audio", "bass_boost"))
        self.soft_limiter = bool(self.config.get("audio", "soft_limiter"))
        
        # Safe volume parsing: heal old 0-256 scale if present in saved JSON
        raw_vol = self.config.get("audio", "volume")
        try:
            val = float(raw_vol) if raw_vol is not None else 0.5
            if val > 2.0: # Detect and heal old 0-256 integer scale
                val = 0.5
            self.volume_gain = max(0.0, min(val, 1.5))
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            self.volume_gain = 0.5

    def process(self, audio_data: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        if audio_data is None or len(audio_data) == 0:
            return audio_data

        data = audio_data.astype(np.float32)
        left = data[:, 0]
        right = data[:, 1]

        # 1. Low-Pass Filter (LPF)
        if self.lpf_mode in self.FIR_KERNELS and self.lpf_mode != "off":
            kernel = self.FIR_KERNELS[self.lpf_mode]
            left = np.convolve(left, kernel, mode='same')
            right = np.convolve(right, kernel, mode='same')

        # 2. Bass Boost
        if self.bass_boost:
            bass_kernel = self.FIR_KERNELS["bass"]
            bass_l = np.convolve(left, bass_kernel, mode='same')
            bass_r = np.convolve(right, bass_kernel, mode='same')
            left = left + (bass_l * 0.45)
            right = right + (bass_r * 0.45)

        # 3. Stereo Widener (Mid-Side Matrix Expansion)
        if self.stereo_widener:
            mid = (left + right) * 0.5
            side = (left - right) * 0.5 * 1.45
            left = mid + side
            right = mid - side

        # 4. Volume Gain (scaled to comfortable master listening volume)
        left *= self.volume_gain
        right *= self.volume_gain

        # 5. Soft Limiter / Anti-Clipping (Analog Tanh Saturation)
        if self.soft_limiter:
            peak = 32767.0
            left = np.tanh(left / peak) * peak
            right = np.tanh(right / peak) * peak

        out = np.empty_like(audio_data)
        out[:, 0] = np.clip(left, -32768, 32767).astype(np.int16)
        out[:, 1] = np.clip(right, -32768, 32767).astype(np.int16)
        return out

# config.py
import os
import json
import copy

CONFIG_FILE = "mgba_config.json"

DEFAULT_CONFIG = {
    "audio": {
        "mute": False,
        "volume": 0.5,
        "low_pass_filter": "medium",
        "stereo_widener": True,
        "bass_boost": False,
        "soft_limiter": True
    },
    "emulation": {
        "skip_bios": True,
        "fast_forward_ratio": 2.0,
        "state_slot": 1,
        "pause_on_unfocus": True
    },
    "sensors": {
        "solar_level": 5,
        "rumble_enabled": True,
        "rumble_strength": 1.0,
        "gyro_enabled": True,
        "gyro_sensitivity": 1.0,
        "mouse_gyro": True,
        "show_hud": True
    },
    "video": {
        "frameskip": 0,
        "interframe_blending": False,
        "scale": 3,
        "window_width": 720,
        "window_height": 649,
        "splitter_sizes": [480, 34, 135]
    },
    "ai_translation": {
        "source_lang": "ja",
        "target_lang": "vi",
        "overlay_enabled": True
    },
    "recent_files": [],
    "paths": {
        "screenshots": "screenshots",
        "saves": "saves",
        "states": "states",
        "cheats": "cheats",
        "exports": "exports",
        "translations": "translations",
        "replays": "replays"
    },
    "input": {
        "keyboard": {
            "b": "x", "a": "z",
            "start": "Return", "select": "Tab",
            "up": "Up", "down": "Down",
            "left": "Left", "right": "Right",
            "l": "a", "r": "s",
            "autofire_a": "c",
            "autofire_b": "v",
            "autofire_l": "d",
            "autofire_r": "f",
            "fast_forward": "=",
            "fast_forward_down": "-",
            "solar_brighten": "]",
            "solar_darken": "[",
            "tilt_left": "q",
            "tilt_right": "e"
        },
        "gamepad": {
            "enabled": True,
            "device_index": 0,
            "deadzone": 0.2,
            "b": "button_0",
            "a": "button_1",
            "select": "button_6",
            "start": "button_7",
            "up": "hat_0_up",
            "down": "hat_0_down",
            "left": "hat_0_left",
            "right": "hat_0_right",
            "l": "button_4",
            "r": "button_5",
            "autofire_a": "button_2",
            "autofire_b": "button_3",
            "autofire_l": "button_9",
            "autofire_r": "button_10",
            "solar_darken": "button_14",
            "solar_brighten": "button_15"
        }
    }
}


class EmulatorConfig:
    def __init__(self):
        self.settings = copy.deepcopy(DEFAULT_CONFIG)
        self.load()

    def load(self):
        if os.path.exists(CONFIG_FILE):
            try:
                with open(CONFIG_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    user_config = json.load(f)

                    vol = user_config.get("audio", {}).get("volume", 0.5)
                    if vol is not None and float(vol) > 2.0:
                        user_config["audio"]["volume"] = 0.5

                    if "input" in user_config and "keyboard" not in user_config["input"]:
                        old_keys = user_config["input"]
                        user_config["input"] = {
                            "keyboard": old_keys,
                            "gamepad": DEFAULT_CONFIG["input"]["gamepad"]
                        }

                    self._update_dict(self.settings, user_config)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Config load error: {e}")
        self.save()

    def _update_dict(self, base, new):
        for k, v in new.items():
            if isinstance(v, dict) and k in base:
                self._update_dict(base[k], v)
            else:
                base[k] = v

    def save(self):
        for path_name, path_val in self.settings.get("paths", {}).items():
            os.makedirs(path_val, exist_ok=True)

        try:
            with open(CONFIG_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(self.settings, f, indent=4)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Config save error: {e}")

    def reset_to_defaults(self):
        self.settings = copy.deepcopy(DEFAULT_CONFIG)
        self.save()

    def get(self, section, key=None, default=None):
        """Safely retrieves config values, accepting an optional default argument."""
        if key is None:
            return self.settings.get(section, default)
        sec = self.settings.get(section)
        if isinstance(sec, dict):
            return sec.get(key, default)
        return default

    def set(self, section, key, value):
        if section not in self.settings:
            self.settings[section] = {}
        self.settings[section][key] = value
        self.save()

    def get_recent_files(self):
        recent = self.settings.get("recent_files", [])
        return [f for f in recent if os.path.exists(f)]

    def add_recent_file(self, filepath: str):
        if not filepath: return
        filepath = os.path.abspath(filepath)
        recent = self.settings.get("recent_files", [])
        if filepath in recent:
            recent.remove(filepath)
        recent.insert(0, filepath)
        self.settings["recent_files"] = recent[:10]
        self.save()

    def clear_recent_files(self):
        self.settings["recent_files"] = []
        self.save()

# controller.py
import os
import sys
import ctypes
os.environ["PYGAME_HIDE_SUPPORT_PROMPT"] = "1"
import pygame

from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QDialog, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, 
                             QLabel, QPushButton, QComboBox, QTableWidget, 
                             QTableWidgetItem, QHeaderView, QMessageBox, QGroupBox)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer

GBA_BUTTON_NAMES = [
    ("a", "A Button"),
    ("b", "B Button"),
    ("l", "L Shoulder"),
    ("r", "R Shoulder"),
    ("start", "Start"),
    ("select", "Select"),
    ("up", "D-Pad Up"),
    ("down", "D-Pad Down"),
    ("left", "D-Pad Left"),
    ("right", "D-Pad Right"),
    ("solar_brighten", "Brighten Solar Sensor (R3)"),
    ("solar_darken", "Darken Solar Sensor (L3)"),
    ("autofire_a", "Autofire A (Turbo)"),
    ("autofire_b", "Autofire B (Turbo)"),
    ("autofire_l", "Autofire L (Turbo)"),
    ("autofire_r", "Autofire R (Turbo)")
]

LIBRETRO_BTNS = {
    "b": 0, "a": 8, "select": 2, "start": 3,
    "up": 4, "down": 5, "left": 6, "right": 7,

    "solar_darken": 14,
    "solar_brighten": 15
}

AUTOFIRE_ACTIONS = ["autofire_a", "autofire_b", "autofire_l", "autofire_r"]

# =============================================================================
# Windows Native XInput Vibration Definitions (Explicit 64-bit Safe C-Types)
# =============================================================================
class XINPUT_VIBRATION(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [
        ("wLeftMotorSpeed", ctypes.c_ushort),
        ("wRightMotorSpeed", ctypes.c_ushort)
    ]


class ControllerManager:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        self.joysticks = {}
        self.initialized = False
        self._xinput_dll = None
        self._init_subsystem()
        self._init_xinput()

        self._rumble_stop_timer = QTimer()
        self._rumble_stop_timer.setSingleShot(True)
        self._rumble_stop_timer.timeout.connect(self.stop_rumble)

    def _init_xinput(self):
        """Loads and configures Windows XInput with explicit 64-bit argument typing."""
        if sys.platform.startswith("win"):
            for name in ["xinput1_4.dll", "xinput1_3.dll", "xinput9_1_0.dll"]:
                try:
                    dll = ctypes.windll.LoadLibrary(name)
                    if hasattr(dll, "XInputSetState"):
                        dll.XInputSetState.argtypes = [ctypes.c_uint, ctypes.POINTER(XINPUT_VIBRATION)]
                        dll.XInputSetState.restype = ctypes.c_uint
                        self._xinput_dll = dll
                        print(f"[RUMBLE DEBUG] Loaded native Windows vibration driver: {name}")
                        break
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"[RUMBLE DEBUG] Failed loading {name}: {e}")

    def _init_subsystem(self):
        try:
            if not pygame.get_init():
                pygame.init()
            if not pygame.joystick.get_init():
                pygame.joystick.init()
            self.initialized = True
            devs = self.rescan_devices()
            print(f"[CONTROLLER DEBUG] Initialized SDL subsystem. Found {len(devs)} device(s): {devs}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[CONTROLLER DEBUG] Initialization error: {e}")

    def rescan_devices(self):
        if not self.initialized: return []
        pygame.joystick.quit()
        pygame.joystick.init()
        self.joysticks.clear()
        
        count = pygame.joystick.get_count()
        devices = []
        for i in range(count):
            try:
                js = pygame.joystick.Joystick(i)
                js.init()
                self.joysticks[i] = js
                devices.append(f"{i}: {js.get_name()}")
            except Exception: pass
        return devices

    def trigger_rumble(self, low_freq: float = 0.8, high_freq: float = 0.8, duration_ms: int = 150):
        """Sends vibration signals to physical gamepad motors and logs diagnostic state."""
        if not self.config.get("sensors", "rumble_enabled"):
            return False

        if low_freq <= 0.0 and high_freq <= 0.0:
            self.stop_rumble()
            return True

        strength = float(self.config.get("sensors", "rumble_strength") or 1.0)
        low = max(0.0, min(1.0, low_freq * strength))
        high = max(0.0, min(1.0, high_freq * strength))

        vibrated = False
        xinput_slots_success = []

        # 1. Native Windows XInput (probe all 4 controller player slots)
        if self._xinput_dll:
            vib = XINPUT_VIBRATION()
            vib.wLeftMotorSpeed = int(low * 65535)
            vib.wRightMotorSpeed = int(high * 65535)
            for user_idx in range(4):
                try:
                    res = self._xinput_dll.XInputSetState(user_idx, ctypes.byref(vib))
                    if res == 0:  # ERROR_SUCCESS
                        vibrated = True
                        xinput_slots_success.append(user_idx)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            if vibrated:
                self._rumble_stop_timer.start(max(75, duration_ms))

        # 2. Pygame / SDL2 Fallback: Run only if XInput did not claim the device
        sdl_vibrated = []
        if not xinput_slots_success:
            for idx, js in self.joysticks.items():
                if hasattr(js, 'rumble'):
                    try:
                        if js.rumble(low, high, duration_ms):
                            vibrated = True
                            sdl_vibrated.append(f"{idx} ({js.get_name()})")
                    except Exception:
                        pass

        # Diagnostic Debug Logging
        if vibrated:
            status_parts = []
            if xinput_slots_success:
                status_parts.append(f"XInput slot(s) {xinput_slots_success}")
            if sdl_vibrated:
                status_parts.append(f"SDL device(s) {sdl_vibrated}")
            print(f"[RUMBLE HARDWARE] Motor active -> Low: {low:.2f}, High: {high:.2f}, Dur: {duration_ms}ms on {', '.join(status_parts)}")
        else:
            print(f"[RUMBLE HARDWARE WARNING] Trigger called (L:{low:.2f}, H:{high:.2f}) but no physical gamepad accepted vibration!")

        return vibrated

    def stop_rumble(self):
        if self._xinput_dll:
            vib = XINPUT_VIBRATION(0, 0)
            for user_idx in range(4):
                try:
                    self._xinput_dll.XInputSetState(user_idx, ctypes.byref(vib))
                except Exception:
                    pass

        for js in self.joysticks.values():
            if hasattr(js, 'stop_rumble'):
                try: js.stop_rumble()
                except Exception: pass

    def _read_input_element(self, js, mapped_input, deadzone):
        if not mapped_input: return False
        
        if str(mapped_input).startswith("button_"):
            try:
                btn_num = int(mapped_input.split("_")[1])
                if btn_num < js.get_numbuttons():
                    return bool(js.get_button(btn_num))
            except Exception: pass

        elif str(mapped_input).startswith("hat_"):
            try:
                parts = mapped_input.split("_")
                hat_num, direction = int(parts[1]), parts[2]
                if hat_num < js.get_numhats():
                    hx, hy = js.get_hat(hat_num)
                    if direction == "up": return (hy > 0)
                    elif direction == "down": return (hy < 0)
                    elif direction == "left": return (hx < 0)
                    elif direction == "right": return (hx > 0)
            except Exception: pass

        elif str(mapped_input).startswith("axis_"):
            try:
                parts = mapped_input.split("_")
                axis_num, sign = int(parts[1]), parts[2]
                if axis_num < js.get_numaxes():
                    val = js.get_axis(axis_num)
                    if sign == "+": return (val > deadzone)
                    elif sign == "-": return (val < -deadzone)
            except Exception: pass
        return False

    def poll_gamepad_events(self, worker):
        if not self.initialized or not worker: return
        pygame.event.pump()

        gamepad_cfg = self.config.get("input", "gamepad")
        if not gamepad_cfg or not gamepad_cfg.get("enabled", True): return

        device_idx = gamepad_cfg.get("device_index", 0)
        js = self.joysticks.get(device_idx)
        if not js: return

        deadzone = float(gamepad_cfg.get("deadzone", 0.2))

        # 1. Standard Buttons & Solar Hotkeys
        for gba_key, action_id in LIBRETRO_BTNS.items():
            mapped_input = gamepad_cfg.get(gba_key)
            is_pressed = self._read_input_element(js, mapped_input, deadzone)
            worker.set_input(action_id, is_pressed)

        # 2. Autofire (Turbo)
        for af_key in AUTOFIRE_ACTIONS:
            target_btn = af_key.replace("autofire_", "")
            mapped_input = gamepad_cfg.get(af_key)
            is_pressed = self._read_input_element(js, mapped_input, deadzone)
            worker.set_autofire(target_btn, is_pressed)

        # 3. Analog Sticks -> Motion / Gyro / Tilt Sensor Simulation
        num_axes = js.get_numaxes()
        if num_axes >= 2:
            lx = js.get_axis(0)
            ly = js.get_axis(1)
            raw_x = 0.0 if abs(lx) < deadzone else lx
            raw_y = 0.0 if abs(ly) < deadzone else ly

            if num_axes >= 4:
                rx = js.get_axis(2 if num_axes == 4 else 3)
                if abs(rx) >= deadzone:
                    raw_x = rx

            worker.set_analog_motion(raw_x, raw_y)


class KeyCaptureDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, mode="keyboard", joystick=None, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.mode = mode
        self.joystick = joystick
        self.captured_value = None
        
        self.setWindowTitle("Press Key or Button")
        self.resize(320, 130)
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        msg = "Press any KEY on your keyboard..." if mode == "keyboard" else "Press any BUTTON on controller..."
        self.label = QLabel(msg)
        self.label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self.label.setStyleSheet("font-size: 14px; font-weight: bold;")
        layout.addWidget(self.label)

        cancel_btn = QPushButton("Cancel")
        cancel_btn.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        layout.addWidget(cancel_btn)

        if self.mode == "gamepad" and self.joystick:
            self.poll_timer = QTimer(self)
            self.poll_timer.timeout.connect(self._poll_controller)
            self.poll_timer.start(20)

    def keyPressEvent(self, event):
        if self.mode == "keyboard":
            if event.key() == Qt.Key.Key_Escape:
                self.reject()
                return

            SPECIAL_KEYS = {
                Qt.Key.Key_Up: "up",
                Qt.Key.Key_Down: "down",
                Qt.Key.Key_Left: "left",
                Qt.Key.Key_Right: "right",
                Qt.Key.Key_Return: "return",
                Qt.Key.Key_Enter: "return",
                Qt.Key.Key_Tab: "tab",
                Qt.Key.Key_Space: "space",
                Qt.Key.Key_Backspace: "backspace",
                Qt.Key.Key_Shift: "shift",
                Qt.Key.Key_Control: "ctrl",
                Qt.Key.Key_Alt: "alt",
                Qt.Key.Key_BracketLeft: "[",
                Qt.Key.Key_BracketRight: "]",
                Qt.Key.Key_Minus: "-",
                Qt.Key.Key_Equal: "="
            }

            if event.key() in SPECIAL_KEYS:
                self.captured_value = SPECIAL_KEYS[event.key()]
                self.accept()
                return

            key_name = event.text().lower()
            if key_name:
                self.captured_value = key_name
                self.accept()
        else:
            super().keyPressEvent(event)

    def _poll_controller(self):
        pygame.event.pump()
        js = self.joystick
        if not js: return

        for b in range(js.get_numbuttons()):
            if js.get_button(b):
                self.captured_value = f"button_{b}"
                self.poll_timer.stop()
                self.accept()
                return

        for h in range(js.get_numhats()):
            hx, hy = js.get_hat(h)
            if hy > 0: self.captured_value = f"hat_{h}_up"
            elif hy < 0: self.captured_value = f"hat_{h}_down"
            elif hx < 0: self.captured_value = f"hat_{h}_left"
            elif hx > 0: self.captured_value = f"hat_{h}_right"
            if self.captured_value:
                self.poll_timer.stop()
                self.accept()
                return


class ControllerConfigDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, config, controller_manager, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.config = config
        self.cm = controller_manager
        self.setWindowTitle("Configure Controls & Motion Feedback")
        self.resize(560, 620)

        self._init_ui()
        self.load_current_mappings()

    def _init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        top_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        top_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Target Device:"))
        self.device_combo = QComboBox()
        self.device_combo.addItem("Keyboard")
        
        devices = self.cm.rescan_devices()
        for dev in devices:
            self.device_combo.addItem(f"Gamepad: {dev}")

        self.device_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_device_changed)
        top_layout.addWidget(self.device_combo)

        btn_rescan = QPushButton("Rescan Controllers")
        btn_rescan.clicked.connect(self.rescan_gamepads)
        top_layout.addWidget(btn_rescan)
        layout.addLayout(top_layout)

        vib_group = QGroupBox("Gamepad Rumble Motor")
        vib_layout = QHBoxLayout(vib_group)
        btn_test_rumble = QPushButton("⚡ Test Motor Vibration")
        btn_test_rumble.setStyleSheet("background-color: #E65100; color: white; font-weight: bold; padding: 4px;")
        btn_test_rumble.clicked.connect(self.test_rumble)
        vib_layout.addWidget(btn_test_rumble)
        layout.addWidget(vib_group)

        self.table = QTableWidget(len(GBA_BUTTON_NAMES), 3)
        self.table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["GBA Action / Sensor", "Assigned Binding", "Remap"])
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
        layout.addWidget(self.table)

        bottom_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_save = QPushButton("Save & Apply")
        btn_save.setStyleSheet("background-color: #4CAF50; color: white; font-weight: bold; padding: 6px;")
        btn_save.clicked.connect(self.save_and_close)
        
        btn_close = QPushButton("Cancel")
        btn_close.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        
        bottom_layout.addStretch()
        bottom_layout.addWidget(btn_save)
        bottom_layout.addWidget(btn_close)
        layout.addLayout(bottom_layout)

    def test_rumble(self):
        print("\n[RUMBLE TEST] Triggering test vibration...")
        success = self.cm.trigger_rumble(low_freq=1.0, high_freq=1.0, duration_ms=400)
        
        # Resolve MainWindow instance through parent hierarchy
        parent_win = self.parent() or self.window()
        if hasattr(parent_win, 'on_manual_rumble_test'):
            parent_win.on_manual_rumble_test()

        if success:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Rumble Test", "Vibration signal sent successfully to gamepad!")
        else:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Rumble Test", "No controller responded to the vibration test. Verify your controller is plugged in and supported.")

    def rescan_gamepads(self):
        current_text = self.device_combo.currentText()
        self.device_combo.clear()
        self.device_combo.addItem("Keyboard")
        devices = self.cm.rescan_devices()
        for dev in devices:
            self.device_combo.addItem(f"Gamepad: {dev}")
        idx = self.device_combo.findText(current_text)
        if idx >= 0: self.device_combo.setCurrentIndex(idx)
        QMessageBox.information(self, "Rescan", f"Found {len(devices)} game controller(s).")

    def on_device_changed(self):
        self.load_current_mappings()

    def load_current_mappings(self):
        is_kb = (self.device_combo.currentIndex() == 0)
        bindings = self.config.get("input", "keyboard" if is_kb else "gamepad") or {}

        for row, (gba_key, label_name) in enumerate(GBA_BUTTON_NAMES):
            self.table.setItem(row, 0, QTableWidgetItem(label_name))
            
            val = str(bindings.get(gba_key, "Unbound"))
            item_val = QTableWidgetItem(val)
            item_val.setTextAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
            self.table.setItem(row, 1, item_val)

            btn = QPushButton("Bind")
            btn.clicked.connect(lambda _, r=row, k=gba_key: self.trigger_rebind(r, k))
            self.table.setCellWidget(row, 2, btn)

    def trigger_rebind(self, row, gba_key):
        is_kb = (self.device_combo.currentIndex() == 0)
        js = None
        if not is_kb:
            dev_idx = max(0, self.device_combo.currentIndex() - 1)
            js = self.cm.joysticks.get(dev_idx)

        dlg = KeyCaptureDialog(mode="keyboard" if is_kb else "gamepad", joystick=js, parent=self)
        if dlg.exec() == QDialog.DialogCode.Accepted and dlg.captured_value:
            new_val = dlg.captured_value
            self.table.item(row, 1).setText(str(new_val))

    def save_and_close(self):
        is_kb = (self.device_combo.currentIndex() == 0)
        target_section = "keyboard" if is_kb else "gamepad"
        current_map = dict(self.config.get("input", target_section) or {})

        for row, (gba_key, _) in enumerate(GBA_BUTTON_NAMES):
            current_map[gba_key] = self.table.item(row, 1).text()

        if not is_kb:
            current_map["device_index"] = max(0, self.device_combo.currentIndex() - 1)
            current_map["enabled"] = True

        self.config.set("input", target_section, current_map)
        self.config.save()
        self.accept()

# core.py
import os
import time
import threading
import numpy as np
from pdretro import Emulator

class EmulatorCore:
    def __init__(self, core_path: str, rom_path: str, config: dict):
        if not os.path.exists(core_path):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"mGBA Core not found at {core_path}")
        if not os.path.exists(rom_path):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"GBA ROM not found at {rom_path}")

        self.emu = Emulator(core_path)
        self.config = config
        self.fast_forward = False
        
        # Configure BIOS based on config dictionary
        skip_bios = self.config.get("skip_bios", True)
        try:
            if hasattr(self.emu, 'options'):
                self.emu.options["mgba_use_bios"] = "ON"
                self.emu.options["mgba_skip_bios"] = "ON" if skip_bios else "OFF"
                
            set_opt = getattr(self.emu, 'set_core_option', getattr(self.emu, 'set_option', None))
            if set_opt:
                set_opt("mgba_use_bios", "ON")
                set_opt("mgba_skip_bios", "ON" if skip_bios else "OFF")  
        except Exception as e:
            print("Failed to set BIOS options:", e)

        self.emu.load_game(rom_path)
        
        # Threading mechanisms
        self.latest_video_frame = None
        self._stop_event = threading.Event()
        self.core_lock = threading.Lock() 
        self.audio_error = None

    def run_loop(self):
        """The main execution thread for the emulator."""
        stream = None
        
        if self.config.get("enable_audio", False):
            sample_rate = 44100 
            try:
                if hasattr(self.emu, 'av_info'):
                    sample_rate = int(self.emu.av_info.sample_rate)
            except Exception: pass

            try:
                import sounddevice as sd
                stream = sd.OutputStream(samplerate=sample_rate, channels=2, dtype='int16', latency='low')
                stream.start()
            except ImportError:
                self.audio_error = "sounddevice module not installed. Audio disabled."
            except Exception as e:
                self.audio_error = f"Audio failed to initialize: {e}"

        gen_source = getattr(self.emu, 'frames', getattr(self.emu, 'video_frames', None))
        if not gen_source: 
            raise RuntimeError("Valid frame generator not found in core!")

        generator = gen_source() if callable(gen_source) else iter(gen_source)
        next_time = time.perf_counter()

        try:
            while not self._stop_event.is_set():
                with self.core_lock:
                    try:
                        item = next(generator)
                    except StopIteration:
                        break 
                
                # Unpack Frame
                if isinstance(item, tuple):
                    vid_obj, aud_obj = item[0], item[1] if len(item) > 1 else None
                else:
                    vid_obj, aud_obj = getattr(item, 'video', item), getattr(item, 'audio', None)
                
                # Assign Video Array
                if hasattr(vid_obj, 'to_rgb'): 
                    self.latest_video_frame = vid_obj.to_rgb()
                elif hasattr(vid_obj, 'data'): 
                    self.latest_video_frame = np.array(vid_obj.data, copy=False)
                else: 
                    self.latest_video_frame = np.array(vid_obj, copy=False)
                    
                # Calculate Framerate
                is_ff = self.fast_forward
                ff_mult = self.config.get("fast_forward_speed", 2.0)
                fps_target = (59.7275 * ff_mult) if is_ff else 59.7275
                frame_time = 1.0 / fps_target
                played_audio = False
                
                # Handle Audio
                if stream and aud_obj is not None and not is_ff:
                    raw_audio = getattr(aud_obj, 'data', aud_obj)
                    if raw_audio is not None and len(raw_audio) > 0:
                        try:
                            audio_arr = np.frombuffer(raw_audio, dtype=np.int16)
                            if len(audio_arr) % 2 == 0:
                                stream.write(audio_arr.reshape(-1, 2))
                                played_audio = True
                        except Exception: pass

                # Sync Clock
                if not played_audio:
                    now = time.perf_counter()
                    sleep_time = next_time - now
                    if sleep_time > 0:
                        time.sleep(sleep_time)
                        next_time += frame_time
                    else:
                        next_time = now + frame_time 

        finally:
            if stream:
                stream.stop()
                stream.close()

    def get_memory_region(self, region_id: int) -> bytes:
        """Safely extracts raw memory from the running core."""
        with self.core_lock:
            try:
                mem = self.emu.get_memory(region_id)
                if mem is not None:
                    if hasattr(mem, 'data'): return bytes(mem.data)
                    elif hasattr(mem, 'read'): return mem.read(0, getattr(mem, 'size', 98304))
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Failed to fetch memory ID {region_id}: {e}")
        return None

    def set_input(self, button_id: int, pressed: bool, port: int = 0):
        with self.core_lock:
            if hasattr(self.emu, 'set_button'):
                self.emu.set_button(port, button_id, pressed)

    def save_state(self, filepath: str):
        with self.core_lock:
            state_bytes = self.emu.save_state()
        with open(filepath, "wb") as f: 
            f.write(state_bytes)

    def load_state(self, filepath: str):
        if not os.path.exists(filepath): return
        with open(filepath, "rb") as f: 
            state_bytes = f.read()
        with self.core_lock: 
            self.emu.load_state(state_bytes)

    def stop(self):
        self._stop_event.set()

    def close(self):
        self.stop()
        with self.core_lock:
            if hasattr(self, 'emu') and self.emu:
                if hasattr(self.emu, 'close'): self.emu.close()
                elif hasattr(self.emu, 'unload_game'): self.emu.unload_game()

--- END OF FILE core.py ---

--- START OF FILE core_worker.py ---

# core_worker.py
import os
import sys
import time
import math
import ctypes
import numpy as np

try:
    from PyQt6.QtCore import QThread, pyqtSignal, QRecursiveMutex as QMutexClass, QMutexLocker, QTimer
except ImportError:
    from PyQt6.QtCore import QThread, pyqtSignal, QMutex as QMutexClass, QMutexLocker, QTimer

from pdretro import Emulator, MemoryRegion
from cheats import CheatEngine
from audio_dsp import AudioDSP
from session_recorder import SessionRecorder, ID_TO_BTN

AF_TO_LIBRETRO = {
    "a": 8,
    "b": 0,
    "l": 10,
    "r": 11
}

# =============================================================================
# Libretro Native Environment & Rumble C-Types Specifications
# =============================================================================
RETRO_ENVIRONMENT_SET_PIXEL_FORMAT = 1
RETRO_ENVIRONMENT_GET_SYSTEM_DIRECTORY = 9
RETRO_ENVIRONMENT_SET_INPUT_DESCRIPTORS = 11
RETRO_ENVIRONMENT_GET_VARIABLE = 15
RETRO_ENVIRONMENT_SET_VARIABLES = 16
RETRO_ENVIRONMENT_GET_VARIABLE_UPDATE = 17
RETRO_ENVIRONMENT_GET_RUMBLE_INTERFACE = 23
RETRO_ENVIRONMENT_GET_LOG_INTERFACE = 27
RETRO_ENVIRONMENT_GET_SAVE_DIRECTORY = 31

class RetroVariable(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [
        ("key", ctypes.c_char_p),
        ("value", ctypes.c_char_p)
    ]

# typedef bool (*retro_set_rumble_state_t)(unsigned port, enum retro_rumble_effect effect, uint16_t strength);
RUMBLE_CB_TYPE = ctypes.CFUNCTYPE(ctypes.c_bool, ctypes.c_uint, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_ushort)

class RetroRumbleInterface(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [
        ("set_rumble_state", RUMBLE_CB_TYPE)
    ]

ENV_CB_TYPE = ctypes.CFUNCTYPE(ctypes.c_bool, ctypes.c_uint, ctypes.c_void_p)


class EmulatorWorker(QThread):
    frame_ready = pyqtSignal(np.ndarray)
    error_occurred = pyqtSignal(str)
    stopped = pyqtSignal()
    replay_finished = pyqtSignal()
    rumble_triggered = pyqtSignal(float, float, int)
    solar_changed = pyqtSignal(int)
    tilt_changed = pyqtSignal(float, float, float)
    game_identified = pyqtSignal(str, str, str)

    def __init__(self, core_path: str, rom_path: str, config):
        super().__init__()
        self.core_path = os.path.abspath(core_path)
        self.rom_path = os.path.abspath(rom_path) if rom_path else ""
        self.config = config

        self.emu = None
        self._is_running = False
        self.fast_forward = False
        self.is_paused = False
        self.step_requested = False
        self.reset_requested = False

        self.mutex = QMutexClass()
        self.cheat_engine = CheatEngine(self)
        self.dsp = AudioDSP(self.config)

        self.recorder = SessionRecorder()
        self.replay_frame_counter = 0

        self.latest_video_frame = None
        self.previous_frame = None
        self._last_gui_render_time = 0.0

        self.standard_buttons = {}
        self.autofire_buttons = {"a": False, "b": False, "l": False, "r": False}

        # Sensor State
        self.solar_level = int(self.config.get("sensors", "solar_level") if self.config.get("sensors") else 5)
        self.tilt_x = 0.0
        self.tilt_y = 0.0
        self.gyro_angle = 0.0

        # Native Libretro C-Types Handlers
        self._core_dll = None
        self._c_environ_cb = None
        self._c_rumble_cb = None
        self._active_rumble_low = 0.0
        self._active_rumble_high = 0.0

        self._c_str_cache = {
            b"mgba_force_gbp": b"ON",
            b"mgba_use_bios": b"OFF",
            b"mgba_skip_bios": b"ON",
            b"mgba_gb_model": b"Autodetect",
            b"mgba_solar_sensor_level": str(self.solar_level).encode("ascii"),
            b"saves_dir": b"saves",
            b"current_dir": b"."
        }

        # Read Cartridge Header (0xAC: Game Code, 0xA0: Title)
        self.game_code = "UNKNOWN"
        self.game_title = "Unknown"
        self.rumble_type = "None"
        self._parse_rom_header()

    def _parse_rom_header(self):
        try:
            if self.rom_path and os.path.exists(self.rom_path):
                with open(self.rom_path, "rb") as f:
                    f.seek(0xAC)
                    self.game_code = f.read(4).decode("latin-1", errors="ignore").strip().upper()
                    f.seek(0xA0)
                    self.game_title = f.read(12).decode("latin-1", errors="ignore").strip()

                if self.game_code in ["BPPE", "BPPJ", "BPPP", "A8NE", "A8NJ", "A8NP", "AX4E", "AX4J", "AX4P"]:
                    self.rumble_type = "GBP_RUMBLE"
                elif self.game_code in ["V49E", "V49J", "V49P", "2GBP"]:
                    self.rumble_type = "CARTRIDGE_RUMBLE"
                elif self.game_code in ["RZWE", "RZWJ", "RZWP"]:
                    self.rumble_type = "RUMBLE_AND_GYRO"
                else:
                    self.rumble_type = "None"
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _libretro_environ_cb(self, cmd: int, data: int) -> bool:
        """Acts as the Libretro environment provider, configuring mGBA natively."""
        if cmd == RETRO_ENVIRONMENT_GET_VARIABLE:
            if not data:
                return False
            var = ctypes.cast(data, ctypes.POINTER(RetroVariable))
            key = var.contents.key
            if not key:
                return False

            if key == b"mgba_force_gbp":
                var.contents.value = self._c_str_cache[b"mgba_force_gbp"]
                return True
            elif key == b"mgba_solar_sensor_level":
                self._c_str_cache[b"mgba_solar_sensor_level"] = str(self.solar_level).encode("ascii")
                var.contents.value = self._c_str_cache[b"mgba_solar_sensor_level"]
                return True
            elif key == b"mgba_use_bios":
                var.contents.value = self._c_str_cache[b"mgba_use_bios"]
                return True
            elif key == b"mgba_skip_bios":
                var.contents.value = self._c_str_cache[b"mgba_skip_bios"]
                return True
            elif key == b"mgba_gb_model":
                var.contents.value = self._c_str_cache[b"mgba_gb_model"]
                return True
            return False

        elif cmd == RETRO_ENVIRONMENT_GET_RUMBLE_INTERFACE:
            if not data:
                return False
            iface = ctypes.cast(data, ctypes.POINTER(RetroRumbleInterface))
            iface.contents.set_rumble_state = self._c_rumble_cb
            return True

        elif cmd == RETRO_ENVIRONMENT_SET_PIXEL_FORMAT:
            return True

        elif cmd == RETRO_ENVIRONMENT_SET_INPUT_DESCRIPTORS:
            return True

        elif cmd == RETRO_ENVIRONMENT_GET_SYSTEM_DIRECTORY:
            if data:
                ptr = ctypes.cast(data, ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_char_p))
                ptr.contents.value = self._c_str_cache[b"current_dir"]
                return True
            return False

        elif cmd == RETRO_ENVIRONMENT_GET_SAVE_DIRECTORY:
            if data:
                ptr = ctypes.cast(data, ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_char_p))
                ptr.contents.value = self._c_str_cache[b"saves_dir"]
                return True
            return False

        return False

    def _libretro_rumble_cb(self, port: int, effect: int, strength: int) -> bool:
        """Dispatches genuine in-game vibration events fired directly by mGBA's CPU core."""
        if not self.config.get("sensors", "rumble_enabled"):
            return True

        norm_val = min(1.0, max(0.0, float(strength) / 65535.0))

        if effect == 0:  # RETRO_RUMBLE_STRONG (low frequency motor)
            self._active_rumble_low = norm_val
        else:            # RETRO_RUMBLE_WEAK (high frequency motor)
            self._active_rumble_high = norm_val

        if self._active_rumble_low <= 0.0 and self._active_rumble_high <= 0.0:
            self.rumble_triggered.emit(0.0, 0.0, 0)
        else:
            self.rumble_triggered.emit(self._active_rumble_low, self._active_rumble_high, 120)

        return True

    def _install_libretro_hooks(self):
        try:
            self._core_dll = ctypes.CDLL(self.core_path)
            if hasattr(self._core_dll, "retro_set_environment"):
                self._c_environ_cb = ENV_CB_TYPE(self._libretro_environ_cb)
                self._c_rumble_cb = RUMBLE_CB_TYPE(self._libretro_rumble_cb)

                self._core_dll.retro_set_environment.argtypes = [ENV_CB_TYPE]
                self._core_dll.retro_set_environment.restype = None
                self._core_dll.retro_set_environment(self._c_environ_cb)
                print("[CORE HOOK] Successfully attached native Libretro environment & rumble interfaces.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[CORE HOOK WARNING] Failed to attach native environment hooks: {e}")

    def update_audio_settings(self):
        if hasattr(self, 'dsp') and self.dsp:
            self.dsp.update_settings()

    def run(self):
        skip_bios = bool(self.config.get("emulation", "skip_bios"))
        self._c_str_cache[b"mgba_skip_bios"] = b"ON" if skip_bios else b"OFF"
        self._c_str_cache[b"mgba_use_bios"] = b"OFF" if skip_bios else b"ON"

        try:
            self.emu = Emulator(self.core_path)
            self._install_libretro_hooks()
            self.emu.load_game(self.rom_path)

            print(f"\n=======================================================")
            print(f"[ROM IDENTIFICATION] Title: {self.game_title} | Code: {self.game_code}")
            print(f"[ROM IDENTIFICATION] Hardware Profile: {self.rumble_type}")
            print(f"[RUMBLE CONFIG] Native Libretro Emulation Active")
            print(f"=======================================================\n")

            self.game_identified.emit(self.game_title, self.game_code, self.rumble_type)

        except Exception as e:
            self.error_occurred.emit(f"Failed to load ROM: {e}")
            return

        self._is_running = True
        stream = None

        if not self.config.get("audio", "mute"):
            sample_rate = 44100
            try:
                if hasattr(self.emu, 'av_info'):
                    sample_rate = int(self.emu.av_info.sample_rate)
                import sounddevice as sd
                stream = sd.OutputStream(samplerate=sample_rate, channels=2, dtype='int16', latency='low')
                stream.start()
            except ImportError:
                self.error_occurred.emit("sounddevice module missing. Audio output disabled.")
            except Exception as e:
                self.error_occurred.emit(f"Audio init failed: {e}")

        gen_source = getattr(self.emu, 'frames', getattr(self.emu, 'video_frames', None))
        if not gen_source:
            self.error_occurred.emit("Frame generator not found in core.")
            return

        generator = gen_source() if callable(gen_source) else iter(gen_source)

        next_time = time.perf_counter()
        frame_counter = 0
        self._last_gui_render_time = time.perf_counter()

        while self._is_running:
            if self.is_paused and not self.step_requested:
                time.sleep(0.02)
                next_time = time.perf_counter()
                continue

            was_single_step = self.step_requested
            if was_single_step:
                self.step_requested = False

            with QMutexLocker(self.mutex):
                if self.reset_requested and hasattr(self.emu, 'reset'):
                    self.emu.reset()
                    self.replay_frame_counter = 0
                    self.reset_requested = False

                self.cheat_engine.apply_cheats()

                # Process Replay vs Normal Input
                if self.recorder.is_playing:
                    pressed_btns, should_pause, is_done = self.recorder.step_playback(self.replay_frame_counter)

                    for libretro_id, btn_name in ID_TO_BTN.items():
                        is_down = (btn_name in pressed_btns)
                        if self.emu and hasattr(self.emu, 'set_button'):
                            self.emu.set_button(0, libretro_id, is_down)

                    if should_pause:
                        self.is_paused = True

                    if is_done:
                        self.replay_finished.emit()

                elif self.recorder.is_recording:
                    active_now = []
                    for libretro_id, btn_name in ID_TO_BTN.items():
                        if self.standard_buttons.get(libretro_id, False):
                            active_now.append(btn_name)

                    self.recorder.record_frame(self.replay_frame_counter, active_now, self.is_paused)

                else:
                    autofire_pulse = (frame_counter // 2) % 2 == 0
                    for btn_name, libretro_id in AF_TO_LIBRETRO.items():
                        is_af_held = self.autofire_buttons.get(btn_name, False)
                        std_held = self.standard_buttons.get(libretro_id, False)

                        if is_af_held:
                            desired = autofire_pulse or std_held
                            if self.emu and hasattr(self.emu, 'set_button'):
                                self.emu.set_button(0, libretro_id, desired)
                        else:
                            if self.emu and hasattr(self.emu, 'set_button'):
                                self.emu.set_button(0, libretro_id, std_held)

                # Send Analog Tilt / Gyro Sensor Axes
                if self.emu:
                    val_x = int(self.tilt_x * 32767)
                    val_y = int(self.tilt_y * 32767)
                    for stick_id in [0, 1]:
                        for meth in ['set_analog', 'set_analog_axis', 'set_axis']:
                            if hasattr(self.emu, meth):
                                try:
                                    getattr(self.emu, meth)(0, stick_id, 0, val_x)
                                    getattr(self.emu, meth)(0, stick_id, 1, val_y)
                                    break
                                except Exception: pass

                self.replay_frame_counter += 1

                try:
                    item = next(generator)
                except StopIteration:
                    break
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"Core step error: {e}")
                    break

            if isinstance(item, tuple):
                vid_obj, aud_obj = item[0], item[1] if len(item) > 1 else None
            else:
                vid_obj, aud_obj = getattr(item, 'video', item), getattr(item, 'audio', None)

            # Video Processing
            frameskip = self.config.get("video", "frameskip") or 0
            render_this_frame = (frame_counter % (frameskip + 1) == 0) or was_single_step
            frame_counter += 1

            if render_this_frame:
                if hasattr(vid_obj, 'to_rgb'): frame_data = vid_obj.to_rgb()
                elif hasattr(vid_obj, 'data'): frame_data = np.array(vid_obj.data, copy=False)
                else: frame_data = np.array(vid_obj, copy=False)

                self.latest_video_frame = frame_data

                if self.config.get("video", "interframe_blending") and not was_single_step:
                    if self.previous_frame is not None and self.previous_frame.shape == frame_data.shape:
                        blended = ((frame_data.astype(np.uint16) + self.previous_frame.astype(np.uint16)) // 2).astype(np.uint8)
                        self.previous_frame = frame_data
                        frame_data = blended
                    else:
                        self.previous_frame = frame_data.copy()

                # =============================================================
                # FAST-FORWARD GUI EVENT QUEUE THROTTLING
                # Caps display updates to ~60 FPS during fast forward so Qt's
                # event loop is never saturated and the UI never freezes.
                # =============================================================
                now_ts = time.perf_counter()
                should_emit_gui = True
                if self.fast_forward and not was_single_step:
                    if (now_ts - self._last_gui_render_time) < 0.016:
                        should_emit_gui = False
                    else:
                        self._last_gui_render_time = now_ts

                if should_emit_gui:
                    self.frame_ready.emit(frame_data)

            # Audio Processing
            is_ff = self.fast_forward
            ff_ratio = self.config.get("emulation", "fast_forward_ratio") or 2.0
            fps_target = (59.7275 * ff_ratio) if is_ff else 59.7275
            frame_time = 1.0 / fps_target
            played_audio = False

            raw_audio = None
            if aud_obj is not None:
                raw_audio = getattr(aud_obj, 'data', aud_obj)

            if raw_audio is not None and len(raw_audio) > 0:
                try:
                    audio_arr = np.frombuffer(raw_audio, dtype=np.int16)
                    if len(audio_arr) % 2 == 0:
                        stereo_audio = audio_arr.reshape(-1, 2)
                        processed_audio = self.dsp.process(stereo_audio)

                        if stream and not is_ff and not was_single_step:
                            stream.write(processed_audio)
                            played_audio = True
                except Exception:
                    pass

            # =============================================================
            # CLOCK PACING FOR NORMAL & FAST-FORWARD SPEED
            # Caps emulation speed to fps_target precisely, eliminating 
            # uncontrolled 1000+ FPS spinlocks that cause "Not Responding".
            # =============================================================
            if not played_audio:
                now = time.perf_counter()
                sleep_time = next_time - now
                if sleep_time > 0.001 and not was_single_step:
                    time.sleep(sleep_time)
                    next_time += frame_time
                else:
                    if sleep_time < -0.05:
                        next_time = now + frame_time
                    else:
                        next_time += frame_time

        if stream:
            stream.stop()
            stream.close()

        with QMutexLocker(self.mutex):
            if self.emu:
                if hasattr(self.emu, 'close'): self.emu.close()
                elif hasattr(self.emu, 'unload_game'): self.emu.unload_game()
                self.emu = None

        self.stopped.emit()

    def set_solar_level(self, level: int):
        level = max(0, min(10, int(level)))
        self.solar_level = level
        self.config.set("sensors", "solar_level", level)
        self.solar_changed.emit(self.solar_level)

    def step_solar_level(self, delta: int):
        new_val = max(0, min(10, self.solar_level + delta))
        if new_val != self.solar_level:
            self.set_solar_level(new_val)
            btn = 15 if delta > 0 else 14
            self.set_input(btn, True)
            QTimer.singleShot(100, lambda: self.set_input(btn, False))

    def set_analog_motion(self, x: float, y: float):
        self.tilt_x = max(-1.0, min(1.0, float(x)))
        self.tilt_y = max(-1.0, min(1.0, float(y)))
        angle = math.degrees(math.atan2(self.tilt_x, -self.tilt_y)) if (self.tilt_x != 0 or self.tilt_y != 0) else 0.0
        self.gyro_angle = angle
        self.tilt_changed.emit(self.tilt_x, self.tilt_y, self.gyro_angle)

    def set_gyro_angle(self, angle_degrees: float):
        self.gyro_angle = float(angle_degrees)
        rad = math.radians(self.gyro_angle)
        self.tilt_x = math.sin(rad)
        self.tilt_y = -math.cos(rad)
        self.tilt_changed.emit(self.tilt_x, self.tilt_y, self.gyro_angle)

    def reset_sensors(self):
        self.set_analog_motion(0.0, 0.0)

    def set_input(self, button_id: int, pressed: bool, port: int = 0):
        with QMutexLocker(self.mutex):
            self.standard_buttons[button_id] = pressed
            if self.emu and hasattr(self.emu, 'set_button'):
                self.emu.set_button(port, button_id, pressed)

    def set_autofire(self, btn_name: str, pressed: bool):
        with QMutexLocker(self.mutex):
            self.autofire_buttons[btn_name] = pressed

    def step_one_frame(self):
        self.is_paused = True
        self.step_requested = True

    def request_reset(self):
        self.reset_requested = True

    def stop(self):
        self._is_running = False

    def save_state(self, filepath: str) -> bool:
        with QMutexLocker(self.mutex):
            if not self.emu: return False

            state_bytes = None
            try:
                import _ra_wrapper
                state_size = _ra_wrapper.get_state_size()
                if state_size > 0:
                    state_bytes = _ra_wrapper.serialize_state()
            except (ImportError, AttributeError):
                try:
                    from pdretro import _ra_wrapper
                    state_size = _ra_wrapper.get_state_size()
                    if state_size > 0:
                        state_bytes = _ra_wrapper.serialize_state()
                except (ImportError, AttributeError): pass

            if state_bytes is None:
                wrapper = getattr(self.emu, '_wrapper', getattr(self.emu, '_ra_wrapper', None))
                if wrapper and hasattr(wrapper, 'serialize_state'):
                    try: state_bytes = wrapper.serialize_state()
                    except Exception: pass

            if state_bytes is None:
                for method_name in ["serialize", "save_state", "get_state", "serialize_state"]:
                    if hasattr(self.emu, method_name):
                        try:
                            state_bytes = getattr(self.emu, method_name)()
                            if state_bytes: break
                        except Exception: pass

            if state_bytes:
                os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(filepath)), exist_ok=True)
                with open(filepath, "wb") as f:
                    f.write(state_bytes)
                return True
            return False

    def load_state(self, filepath: str) -> bool:
        if not os.path.exists(filepath): return False
        with open(filepath, "rb") as f: state_bytes = f.read()
        if not state_bytes: return False

        with QMutexLocker(self.mutex):
            if not self.emu: return False

            try:
                import _ra_wrapper
                if hasattr(_ra_wrapper, 'unserialize_state'):
                    _ra_wrapper.unserialize_state(state_bytes)
                    return True
            except (ImportError, AttributeError):
                try:
                    from pdretro import _ra_wrapper
                    if hasattr(_ra_wrapper, 'unserialize_state'):
                        _ra_wrapper.unserialize_state(state_bytes)
                        return True
                except (ImportError, AttributeError): pass

            wrapper = getattr(self.emu, '_wrapper', getattr(self.emu, '_ra_wrapper', None))
            if wrapper and hasattr(wrapper, 'unserialize_state'):
                try:
                    wrapper.unserialize_state(state_bytes)
                    return True
                except Exception: pass

            for method_name in ["unserialize", "load_state", "set_state", "unserialize_state"]:
                if hasattr(self.emu, method_name):
                    try:
                        getattr(self.emu, method_name)(state_bytes)
                        return True
                    except Exception: pass

            return False

    def get_memory_region(self, region_name) -> bytes:
        with QMutexLocker(self.mutex):
            if not self.emu: return None
            try:
                target_enum = None
                if region_name in ["VRAM", "VIDEO_RAM", 3]:
                    target_enum = getattr(MemoryRegion, 'VIDEO_RAM', 3)
                elif region_name in ["EWRAM", "SYSTEM_RAM", 2]:
                    target_enum = getattr(MemoryRegion, 'SYSTEM_RAM', 2)
                elif region_name in ["SRAM", "SAVE_RAM", 0]:
                    target_enum = getattr(MemoryRegion, 'SAVE_RAM', 0)
                elif region_name in ["RTC", 1]:
                    target_enum = getattr(MemoryRegion, 'RTC', 1)
                elif region_name in ["PRAM", "PALETTE_RAM", 4]:
                    target_enum = getattr(MemoryRegion, 'PALETTE_RAM', 4)
                elif region_name in ["OAM", 5]:
                    target_enum = getattr(MemoryRegion, 'OAM', 5)

                if target_enum is not None:
                    mem = self.emu.get_memory(target_enum)
                    if mem and hasattr(mem, 'data'):
                        return bytes(mem.data)
                    elif mem and hasattr(mem, 'read_bytes'):
                        return mem.read_bytes(0, mem.size)
            except Exception: pass

            if region_name in ["IWRAM"]:
                try:
                    sys_enum = getattr(MemoryRegion, 'SYSTEM_RAM', 2)
                    mem = self.emu.get_memory(sys_enum)
                    if mem and hasattr(mem, 'data'):
                        raw = bytes(mem.data)
                        if len(raw) >= (256 + 32) * 1024:
                            return raw[256 * 1024:(256 + 32) * 1024]
                        return raw[:32 * 1024]
                except Exception: pass

            if region_name in ["ROM", "CARTRIDGE"] and self.rom_path and os.path.exists(self.rom_path):
                try:
                    with open(self.rom_path, 'rb') as f:
                        return f.read()
                except Exception: pass

        return None

    def write_memory_region(self, region_name, offset: int, data: bytes) -> bool:
        with QMutexLocker(self.mutex):
            if not self.emu: return False
            try:
                target_enum = None
                if region_name in ["VRAM", "VIDEO_RAM", 3]:
                    target_enum = getattr(MemoryRegion, 'VIDEO_RAM', 3)
                elif region_name in ["EWRAM", "SYSTEM_RAM", 2]:
                    target_enum = getattr(MemoryRegion, 'SYSTEM_RAM', 2)
                elif region_name in ["SRAM", "SAVE_RAM", 0]:
                    target_enum = getattr(MemoryRegion, 'SAVE_RAM', 0)
                elif region_name in ["RTC", 1]:
                    target_enum = getattr(MemoryRegion, 'RTC', 1)
                elif region_name in ["PRAM", "PALETTE_RAM", 4]:
                    target_enum = getattr(MemoryRegion, 'PALETTE_RAM', 4)
                elif region_name in ["OAM", 5]:
                    target_enum = getattr(MemoryRegion, 'OAM', 5)
                elif region_name in ["IWRAM"]:
                    target_enum = getattr(MemoryRegion, 'SYSTEM_RAM', 2)
                    offset += 256 * 1024

                if target_enum is not None:
                    mem = self.emu.get_memory(target_enum)
                    if mem:
                        if hasattr(mem, 'write_bytes'):
                            mem.write_bytes(offset, data)
                            return True
                        elif hasattr(mem, 'data'):
                            mem.data[offset:offset + len(data)] = np.frombuffer(data, dtype=np.uint8)
                            return True
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Write memory error: {e}")
        return False

    def start_recording_session(self, replays_dir: str = "replays") -> str:
        state_path = self.recorder.start_recording(self, self.rom_path, replays_dir)
        if state_path:
            with QMutexLocker(self.mutex):
                self.replay_frame_counter = 0
        return state_path

    def stop_recording_session(self, replays_dir: str = "replays") -> str:
        with QMutexLocker(self.mutex):
            total_frames = self.replay_frame_counter
        return self.recorder.stop_recording(total_frames, replays_dir)

    def start_replay_session(self, json_path: str) -> bool:
        success = self.recorder.load_replay(json_path, self)
        if success:
            with QMutexLocker(self.mutex):
                self.replay_frame_counter = 0
        return success

    def stop_replay_session(self):
        with QMutexLocker(self.mutex):
            self.recorder.stop_playback()
